# Sources

This file lists every source the wiki cites, one row per key. Pages cite a source in frontmatter as
`<Key> § <locator>` and inline in the body as `[Key]` (schema §9). The validator checks that every key used
in a page is listed here. Vocabulary files in `_vocab\` use the same keys.

## Form

- **Key**: first author surname plus year (`Beck-2021`); two authors joined (`Clark-Wells-1995`); three or more
  use `etal` (`Ehlers-etal-2005`); two works with the same key get `a`, `b` (`Kuyken-etal-2016a`, `Kuyken-etal-2016b`);
  organizations and documents use a short name (`NICE-NG222`, `APA-DSM5TR-2022`); instrument sites end in `-Site`.
  Letters, digits and hyphens only.
- **Kind**: book, article, guideline, manual, instrument, website, standard or law.
- **Access**: a DOI, URL, PMID or ISBN.
- **Notes**: edition, currency, licensing and how much of the source was read (full text, abstract, or
  table of contents only). A note that a source was read only in part is a prompt to recheck it.
- A statement checked by a maintainer against current documentation uses `Maintainer/<YYYY-MM-DD> § <topic>`
  in frontmatter and needs no row here.
- Keep one key per work. Before adding a key, search this table for the DOI or title. Rows are sorted by key.
